<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5882669A-98F2-45E1-9414-85C6288E9096'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jill scores $17$ out of twenty on a test. What percentage is this?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Jill's marks} &= \dfrac {17} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times \cancel {20} \times 5} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                    &= 17 \times 5  \\\\
                    &= 85 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$85 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Jill's marks} &= \dfrac {17} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times \cancel {20} \times 5} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                    &= 17 \times 5  \\\\
                    &= 85 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$85 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out $5 \%$ of $360$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5\% \text { of } 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 360} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 18 \times 10} {2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times 18 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$18$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5\% \text { of } 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 360} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 18 \times 10} {2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times 18 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$18$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out $15 \%$ of $360$.

You can use part (a) to help you.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15\% \text { of } 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 360 \\\\
&= 3 \times \Big(\dfrac {5} {100} \times 360 \Big) \\\\
&= 3 \times 18 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$54$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

&= 3 \times \Big(\dfrac {5} {100} \times 360 \Big) \\\\
&= 3 \times 18 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-DC0AE9E8-3EE0-4BF6-A408-D5E6E1044170'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The table shows whether pupils in a class walk to school.

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


</div>




<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of **boys** walk to school?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% of boys }      &= \dfrac {2} {2 + 8} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &=  2 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$20 \% $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% of boys }      &= \dfrac {2} {2 + 8} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &=  2 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$20 \% $
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of the **pupils** in this class walk to school?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% of pupils }    &= \dfrac {2 + 5} {2 + 8 + 5 + 10} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times 100} {25} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times  25 \times 4} {25} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {25} \times 4} {\cancel {25}}  \\\\
                        &= 7 \times 4  \\\\                                                    
                        &= 28 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$28 \% $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% of pupils }    &= \dfrac {2 + 5} {2 + 8 + 5 + 10} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times 100} {25} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times  25 \times 4} {25} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {25} \times 4} {\cancel {25}}  \\\\
                        &= 7 \times 4  \\\\                                                    
                        &= 28 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$28 \% $
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-211A5FCF-4F61-45F5-A728-04F4F08A4EC3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The table below shows the results of a survey in which $60$ boys 
were asked what colour of dress girls should wear.

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


What percentage of boys thought *girls* should wear pink?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage}   &= \dfrac {24} {24 + 15 + 21} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {24 \times 100} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 6 \times 10 \times 10} {6 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {10} \times  {10}} {\cancel {6} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 10 \\\\
                    &= 40 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage}   &= \dfrac {24} {24 + 15 + 21} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {24 \times 100} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 6 \times 10 \times 10} {6 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {10} \times  {10}} {\cancel {6} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 10 \\\\
                    &= 40 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$40 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-34889FE4-A959-410E-B6B7-7810931125D3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In her examination, Manreet got $16$ marks out of $20$. What was her mark as a percentage?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Manreets mark}               &= \dfrac {16} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 5 \times 20} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 5 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}}   \\\\
                                    &= 16 \times 5  \\\\
                                    &= 80 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$80 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Manreets mark}               &= \dfrac {16} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 5 \times 20} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 5 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}}   \\\\
                                    &= 16 \times 5  \\\\
                                    &= 80 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$80 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find $10 \%$ of $450$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \% \ \text{of} \ 450  \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 450 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 450} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 45 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 45
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$45$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8C475099-0739-4B04-B1D6-BDCA16ACE65F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find $15 \%$ of $240$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text { of } 240 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 240  \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 240} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 4 \times 5 \times 12}{4 \times 5 \times 5}\\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{5} \times 12}{\cancel{4} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}}\\\\
&= 3 \times 12 \\\\
&= 36
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text { of } 240 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 240  \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 240} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 4 \times 5 \times 12}{4 \times 5 \times 5}\\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{5} \times 12}{\cancel{4} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}}\\\\
&= 3 \times 12 \\\\
&= 36
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36$
</code></pre>
</div>

$11 \%$ of $3600 = \text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 11 \% \text{ of } 3600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {11} {100} \times 3600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 3600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 36 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 36 \\\\
&= 396
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$396$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 11 \% \text{ of } 3600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {11} {100} \times 3600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 3600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 36 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 36 \\\\
&= 396
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore} \%$ of $52 = 13$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a} {100} \times 52             &= 13 \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {13 \times 100} {52}  \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {13 \times 4 \times 25} {13 \times 4}  \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{13} \times \cancel{4} \times 25} {\cancel{13} \times \cancel{4}}  \\\\
a                                      &= 25
\end{aligned}
$

Number $= 25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

a                                      &= \dfrac {13 \times 4 \times 25} {13 \times 4}  \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{13} \times \cancel{4} \times 25} {\cancel{13} \times \cancel{4}}  \\\\
a                                      &= 25
\end{aligned}
$

Number $= 25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$15 \%$ of $\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore} = 60$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {15} {100} \times a             &= 60 \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{60 \times 100}{15} \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{15 \times 4 \times 100}{15} \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{\cancel{15} \times 4 \times 100}{\cancel{15}} \\\\
a                                      &= 4 \times 100 \\\\
a                                      &= 400
\end{aligned}
$

Number $= 400$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$400$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

$

\dfrac {15} {100} \times a             &= 60 \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{60 \times 100}{15} \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{15 \times 4 \times 100}{15} \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{\cancel{15} \times 4 \times 100}{\cancel{15}} \\\\
a                                      &= 4 \times 100 \\\\
a                                      &= 400
\end{aligned}
$

Number $= 400$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C2DA9491-EDDE-4593-8A44-2D8FCE3664C6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $15\%$ of $300$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ 300 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times {300} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 15 \times 3 \\\\
&= 45
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$45$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ 300 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times {300} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 15 \times 3 \\\\
&= 45
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$45$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4751D63D-6131-4E1E-BD76-5F3BF5800D72'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $30\%$ of $1600$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \ \text {of} \ 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 16 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 30 \times 16 \\\\
&= 480
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$480$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \ \text {of} \ 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 16 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 30 \times 16 \\\\
&= 480
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8CF1FA33-107D-436D-852E-0527BD6EE663'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of the following has the greatest value?

- $50\%$ of $10$
- $40\%$ of $20$
- $30\%$ of $30$
- $20\%$ of $40$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's calculate the first value.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First value}      &= 50\% \text{ of }  10 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{50}{100} \times 10 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{50 \times 10}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{5\cancel{0}\times 1 \cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0} \cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 5 \\\\
\text{Second value}     &= 40\% \text{ of } \ 20 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{40}{100} \times 20 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{40 \times 20}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4\cancel{0} \times 2\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 8 \\\\
\text{Third value}      &= 30\% \text{ of } \ 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{30}{100} \times 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 30}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{3\cancel{0} \times 3\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 9 \\\\
\text{Fourth value}     &= 20\% \text{ of } \ 40 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{20}{100} \times 40 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{20 \times 40}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2\cancel{0} \times 4\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above,

Biggest value $= 30 \% \text{ of } 30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$30\% \text{ of } \ 30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's calculate the first value.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First value}      &= 50\% \text{ of }  10 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{50}{100} \times 10 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{50 \times 10}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{5\cancel{0}\times 1 \cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0} \cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 5 \\\\
\text{Second value}     &= 40\% \text{ of } \ 20 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{40}{100} \times 20 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{40 \times 20}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4\cancel{0} \times 2\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 8 \\\\
\text{Third value}      &= 30\% \text{ of } \ 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{30}{100} \times 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 30}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{3\cancel{0} \times 3\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 9 \\\\
\text{Fourth value}     &= 20\% \text{ of } \ 40 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{20}{100} \times 40 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{20 \times 40}{100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2\cancel{0} \times 4\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                        &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above,

Biggest value $= 30 \% \text{ of } 30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>A</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$30\% \text{ of } \ 30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0F235D15-061A-4701-8EB6-C7E418A3084D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What percentage of this shape is shaded?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the diagram,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Percentage shaded}   &= \dfrac {3} 5 \times 100 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 20 \\\\
                            &= 60 \%
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$60 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the diagram,

$
\begin {aligned}

                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 20 \\\\
                            &= 60 \%
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B92A2396-20FB-4D73-84F6-53893C6F4B4E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find $4\%$ of $\pounds 5300$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 4 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 5300 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {100} \times {5300} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 53 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 53 \\\\
&= \pounds 212
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 212$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 4 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 5300 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {100} \times {5300} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 53 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 53 \\\\
&= \pounds 212
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9AA2E90D-7CEB-4445-8395-AC73877C9536'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $80\%$ of $\pounds535$

A: $\pounds107$

B: $\pounds372$

C: $\pounds400$

D: $\pounds418$

E: $\pounds428$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {80} {100} \times 535 \\\\
&= \dfrac {80 \times 535} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 5 \times 107} {20 \times 5 } \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 4 \times \cancel{5} \times 107} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} } \\\\
&= 4 \times 107  \\\\
&=  \pounds {428}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 428$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {80} {100} \times 535 \\\\
&= \dfrac {80 \times 535} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 5 \times 107} {20 \times 5 } \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 4 \times \cancel{5} \times 107} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} } \\\\
&= 4 \times 107  \\\\
&=  \pounds {428}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7B55D3DF-EF43-4C91-9AFF-E371FEF2FC3D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find $5\%$ of $24$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \% \text{ of } 24 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 24 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 24} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 4 \times 6} {4 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 4 \times 6} {\cancel 4 \times \cancel 5 \times  5} \\\\
&=\dfrac  {6}{5}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {6} {5}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \% \text{ of } 24 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 24 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 24} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 4 \times 6} {4 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 4 \times 6} {\cancel 4 \times \cancel 5 \times  5} \\\\
&=\dfrac  {6}{5}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {6} {5}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-DE2B4A57-B8DD-4C95-9E77-103AF639A833'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $60\%$ of $10\%$ of $900$ ? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 60 \% \ \text {of} \ 10 \% \ \text {of} \ 900 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60} {100} \times \dfrac {10} {100} \times 900 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60 \times 10 \times 900} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \cancel{0} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 9 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0} \times 1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 6 \times 9 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$54$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 60 \% \ \text {of} \ 10 \% \ \text {of} \ 900 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60} {100} \times \dfrac {10} {100} \times 900 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60 \times 10 \times 900} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \cancel{0} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 9 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0} \times 1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 6 \times 9 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$54$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CAE549F8-9EE0-4076-8DD3-5385034616A6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $6\%$ of six plus $8\%$ of eight. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 6 \% \times 6 + 8 \% \times 8  \\\\
&= \dfrac{6} {100} \times 6 + \dfrac{8} {100} \times 8  \\\\
&= \dfrac{6 \times 6} {100} + \dfrac{8 \times 8} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36} {100} + \dfrac{64} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36 + 64} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {100}} {\cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 1
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 6 \% \times 6 + 8 \% \times 8  \\\\
&= \dfrac{6} {100} \times 6 + \dfrac{8} {100} \times 8  \\\\
&= \dfrac{6 \times 6} {100} + \dfrac{8 \times 8} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36} {100} + \dfrac{64} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36 + 64} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {100}} {\cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 1
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-80F4D32D-E7F6-4E7A-9776-DC2204D80BCA'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $7\%$ of seven plus $9\%$ of nine. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 7 \% \ \text{of} \ 7 + 9 \% \ \text{of} \ 9 \\\\
&= 7 \% \times 7 + 9 \% \times 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac{7 \times 7}{100} + \dfrac{9 \times 9}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{49}{100} + \dfrac{81}{100} \\\\  
&= \dfrac{49 + 81}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{130}{100} \\\\
&= 1.3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 7 \% \ \text{of} \ 7 + 9 \% \ \text{of} \ 9 \\\\
&= 7 \% \times 7 + 9 \% \times 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac{7 \times 7}{100} + \dfrac{9 \times 9}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{49}{100} + \dfrac{81}{100} \\\\  
&= \dfrac{49 + 81}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{130}{100} \\\\
&= 1.3
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 60$

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 6 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 6
\end{aligned}
$

\end{aligned}

&= \dfrac {15 \times 2 \times \cancel{2} \times 1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} {\cancel{2} \times 1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
&= 15 \times 2 \\\\
&= \pounds 30
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2FBBFEB4-886D-4E5F-A72B-7176F96FCF55'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Add together

$25\%$ of $20$?

$\dfrac{1}{5}$ of $30$

and $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of $18$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 25\% \ \text{of} \ 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \ \text{of} \ 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \ \text{of} \ 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \times 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \times 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 20} {100} + \dfrac{30} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 18} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 5} {25 \times 4} + \dfrac{5 \times 6} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times 6} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4} \times 5} {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} + \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 6} {\cancel{5}} + \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{3} \times 6} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 5 + 6 + 2 \times 6 \\\\
&= 11 + 12 \\\\
&= 23
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$23$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 25\% \ \text{of} \ 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \ \text{of} \ 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \ \text{of} \ 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \times 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \times 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 20} {100} + \dfrac{30} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 18} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 5} {25 \times 4} + \dfrac{5 \times 6} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times 6} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4} \times 5} {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} + \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 6} {\cancel{5}} + \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{3} \times 6} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 5 + 6 + 2 \times 6 \\\\
&= 11 + 12 \\\\
&= 23
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$23$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

In a survey $30\%$ of people were left handed only, $10\%$ were both left and right handed. What percentage were right handed only ?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {\% Right handed only}    &= 100 - 30 - 10 \\\\
                                &= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>

                                &= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find $50\%$ of $80$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 50 \% \text { of } 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {50}{100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times 80 }{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \cancel {0} \times 8 \cancel{0} } {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 5 \times 8 \\\\
&= 40 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

&= 5 \times 8 \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

</div>

&= 5 \times 16 \\\\
&= 80
\end{aligned}

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F73B4CF0-A753-41AA-B759-D42A1107AB86'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find  $35\%$ $of$ $80\,kg$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35\% \ \text{of}\ 80 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 80} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 5 \times 20 \times 4}{5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 20 \times 4}{\cancel 5 \times \cancel 20} \\\\
&= 7 \times 4 \\\\
&= 28 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$28 \ \text{kg}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35\% \ \text{of}\ 80 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 80} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 5 \times 20 \times 4}{5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 20 \times 4}{\cancel 5 \times \cancel 20} \\\\
&= 7 \times 4 \\\\
&= 28 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$28 \ \text{kg}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of the large grid on the left is shaded?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% Shaded}    &= \dfrac {\text{Shaded squares}} {\text{Total squares}} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$75 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                    &= 75 \%

$
</code></pre>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What fraction of the small grid on the right is shaded? Give your answer in its simplest form. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction Shaded}  &= \dfrac {\text{Shaded squares}} {\text{Total squares}} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {9} {24} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3 \times 3} {8 \times 3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 8} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3} {8}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {8}$

</div>

                        &=  \dfrac {3 \times 3} {8 \times 3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 8} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4856BEAB-6A07-4C28-8CA2-6773788E9F82'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $60\%$ of $765$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{60}{100} \times 765 \\\\
&= \dfrac{60 \times 765}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{45900}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{459\cancel{00}}{1\cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 459
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$459$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{60}{100} \times 765 \\\\
&= \dfrac{60 \times 765}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{45900}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{459\cancel{00}}{1\cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 459
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1F54DF7F-1E4E-4CDC-A7A2-5D50C56188C5'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Emily scores $30$ out of $60$ in a test.

Hebe scores $60\%$ in the same test.

How many more marks did Hebe get than Emily?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference in marks}   &= \text{Hebes mark's} - \text{Emily's marks} \\\\
\text {Hebe score}            &= 60\% \ \text{of} \ 60 - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {60} {100} \times 60 - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {60 \times 60} {100} - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {6 \cancel{0} \times 6 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} -30 \\\\
                              &=36 - 30 \\\\
                              &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

Marks that Hebe scored more than Emily $=6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference in marks}   &= \text{Hebes mark's} - \text{Emily's marks} \\\\
\text {Hebe score}            &= 60\% \ \text{of} \ 60 - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {60} {100} \times 60 - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {60 \times 60} {100} - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {6 \cancel{0} \times 6 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} -30 \\\\
                              &=36 - 30 \\\\
                              &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

Marks that Hebe scored more than Emily $=6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-007B2641-042F-43E0-8E20-0BE481209612'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What number is $40\%$ of $75$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 40\%$ \ \text{of} \ 75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {100} \times 75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 75} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 10 \times 3 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{4} \times 10 \times 3 \times \cancel{25}} {\cancel{4} \times \cancel{25}} \\\\
&= 10 \times 3 \\\\
&= 30 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 40\%$ \ \text{of} \ 75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {100} \times 75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 75} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 10 \times 3 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{4} \times 10 \times 3 \times \cancel{25}} {\cancel{4} \times \cancel{25}} \\\\
&= 10 \times 3 \\\\
&= 30 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C35C7538-5250-4091-9E22-93D8F8122AC5'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $11\%$ of $32600$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{11}{100} \times 32600 \\\\
&= \dfrac{11 \times 32600}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{11 \times 326\cancel{00}}{1\cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 326 \\\\
&= 3586
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3586$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{11}{100} \times 32600 \\\\
&= \dfrac{11 \times 32600}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{11 \times 326\cancel{00}}{1\cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 326 \\\\
&= 3586
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3FA521CB-71B5-46C2-9B7C-6CE326C735EF'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What percentage of the grid below is shaded? You can assume all boxes are identical in size.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

Total number of boxes $=25$

Number of shaded boxes $= 17$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of shaded grid}    &= \dfrac{\text{Shaded boxes}}{\text{Total boxes}} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac{17}{25}
\en{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find $35 \%$ of $160$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35 \% \ \text{of} \ 160  \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times 160 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 160} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56 \times 100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56 \times \cancel{100}} {\cancel{100}} \\\\
&= {56} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$56$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35 \% \ \text{of} \ 160  \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times 160 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 160} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56 \times 100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56 \times \cancel{100}} {\cancel{100}} \\\\
&= {56} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What is $0.78$ as a percentage? 


- $7.8   \%$
- $78    \%$
- $0.78  \%$
- $780   \%$
- $0.078 \%$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  0.78 \times \dfrac {100}{100} \\\\
&=  (0.78 \times 100) \times \dfrac{1}{100} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {78}{100} \\\\
&=  78\%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$78\%$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  0.78 \times \dfrac {100}{100} \\\\
&=  (0.78 \times 100) \times \dfrac{1}{100} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {78}{100} \\\\
&=  78\%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>B</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E03AFD46-F8E5-4DA8-9B71-1BF8355CF385'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find $30 \%$ of $120$

- $30$
- $3.6$
- $36$ 
- $40$ 
- $64$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&=   30\% \ \text{of} \ 120 \\\\
&=   \dfrac{30}{100} \times 120 \\\\
&=   \dfrac{30 \times 120}{100} \\\\
&=   \dfrac{3 \cancel{0} \times 12 \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&=   3  \times 12  \\\\
&=   36
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&=   30\% \ \text{of} \ 120 \\\\
&=   \dfrac{30}{100} \times 120 \\\\
&=   \dfrac{30 \times 120}{100} \\\\
&=   \dfrac{3 \cancel{0} \times 12 \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&=   3  \times 12  \\\\
&=   36
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>C</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$36$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-535CC06F-08C9-458B-B841-4C448F5D0876'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write three-fifths as a percentage.

- $6 \%$
- $30 \%$
- $35 \%$
- $3 \%$
- $60 \%$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{three-fifths as a percentage} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3}{5} \times 100 \% \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 100}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 20 \times 5}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 20 \\\\
&= 60\%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$60 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{three-fifths as a percentage} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3}{5} \times 100 \% \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 100}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 20 \times 5}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 20 \\\\
&= 60\%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$60 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C82E5C30-82DD-4D11-B4AF-0C2607D4E161'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the difference between $20 \%$ of $90$ and $90 \%$ of $20$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's calculate the first value.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 90 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 90} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 5 \times 18} {20 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {5} \times 18} {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the second value.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 90 \% \ \text {of} \ 20 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 20 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90 \times 20} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {18 \times 5 \times 20} {5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {18 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text {first value} - \text {second value} \\\\
&= 18 - 18 \\\\
&= 0
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's calculate the first value.

\begin{aligned}
&= 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 90 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 90} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 5 \times 18} {20 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {5} \times 18} {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the second value.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 90 \% \ \text {of} \ 20 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 20 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90 \times 20} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {18 \times 5 \times 20} {5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {18 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text {first value} - \text {second value} \\\\
&= 18 - 18 \\\\
&= 0
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-8F86C566-74D4-41CB-ADC6-2648BC6CBC05'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Phil holds a bag containing $16$ blue sweets and $4$ red sweets.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What **percentage** of sweets in this bag are red?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Percentage red sweets}       &= \dfrac {4} {16 + 4} \ \times \ 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {20} \times 5} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 5 \\\\
                                    &= 20 \%   
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$20 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Percentage red sweets}       &= \dfrac {4} {16 + 4} \ \times \ 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {20} \times 5} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 5 \\\\
                                    &= 20 \%   
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$20 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Phil now eats $$4$$ of the blue sweets.

What fraction of the sweets in the bag are now blue?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction of blue sweets}  &= \dfrac {\text{Blue sweets}} {\text{Total sweets left}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 - 4} {20 - 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 3} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 3} {\cancel {4} \times 4} \\\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction of blue sweets}  &= \dfrac {\text{Blue sweets}} {\text{Total sweets left}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 - 4} {20 - 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 3} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 3} {\cancel {4} \times 4} \\\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {3} {4}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
